Coordinate descent on simplex with **pair swaps** could be powerful for n=512. Let's design:
Objective C(a)=max c_k, S fixed. Move mass from coordinate i to j (or split i,j). For a pair (i,j), vary δ: a_i'=a_i+δ, a_j'=a_j-δ, δ ∈ [-a_j, a_i]. Other c_k constants except those involving i or j. Need minimize max of many piecewise linear? Actually c_k changes linearly in δ? Since both a_i,a_j linear, c_k involving a_i*a_j includes (a_i+δ)(a_j-δ) quadratic; involving one is linear. Number affected O(n). We can find δ that minimizes max by evaluating candidate breakpoints where two affected coefficients cross current max. Too complex but doable for random pairs.

Simpler coordinate update: For single coordinate i, vary t = a_i', scale all others to keep sum? Or fix sum by shifting an infinitesimal average (rare). Better use pair swaps to preserve sum without scaling all.

Pair swap local optimization:
- Pick i,j. Let x=a_i, y=a_j. For δ in interval [ -y, x ], new xi=x+δ, xj=y-δ. Other coordinates fixed.
- Compute affected base contributions: For each k, c_k = base_k + linear δ terms + quadratic δ*coeff if c_k includes xi*xj.
   General c_k = ∑_{p+q=k} a_p a_q.
   Changing i,j:
   Terms:
   - p=i,q=r (r≠j): contribution (x+δ) a_r => base + δ a_r.
   - p=r,q=i: a_r(x+δ) => δ a_r.
So 2δ * L_k where L_k = sum of a_r over r such that r+k=i or i+k=r? Actually derivative coefficient = 2 ∑_{r} a_r w_{i+r} (with w delta at k) as before for one coordinate. For two coordinates i,j, changes:
   c_k(δ)=base_k + A_i(k) δ_i + A_j(k) δ_j + (if i+j=k) δ_i δ_j*2? Wait full convolution term a_i a_j appears twice if i≠j and k=i+j: contribution 2(x+δ)(y+δ') with δ_j=-δ. So quadratic.
Specifically c_k = base_k + α_k δ - β_k δ? since δ_j=-δ + quadratic γ_k δ^2.
Where α_k = 2 ∑_r (a_r if r+k=i or i+k=r, excluding j?) - 2∑_r(a_r if r+k=j or j+k=r) plus adjustments for cross term.
Could compute by direct updating affected c: There are O(n) coefficients affected by i or j: all k from i or j to i+n-1/j+n-1. For pair, we can compute new c values for those indices from scratch O(n) using current arrays: for each k in affected set, compute new c_k? Direct O(n^2) if for all. But we can update formula with precomputed neighbor sums.
Maybe too heavy but n=512, random pairs many could work.

Let's design smooth objective for gradient:
Given a sum S, c=conv(a). Define weights w for peaks. Options:
- logsumexp β: w_k = exp((c_k - maxc)/β)/Z. β adaptive: start τ = 0.1*maxc, decrease? Smooth max. Gradient of smooth max P = β logsumexp(c/β). Derivative of P wrt a_i = β * w_i? Actually P = logsumexp(c/β)? If P=β LSE(c/β), ∂P/∂c_k = β w_k. Then Qa = 2 * cross_corr(β w, a). C=P/S^2. log gradient h = a_i Qa_i/S^2 - 2P a_i^2/S^3. We can compute.
- L_p: P=(∑ c^p)^{1/p}; weights ∝ c^{p-1}. Might emphasize peaks less for p moderate.
- Top-K average: w=1/K for top K; derivative step.

Use Adam:
    m = β1 m + (1-β1) grad
    v = β2 v + (1-β2) grad^2
    grad_hat = m/(1-β1^t), v_hat...
    log_a -= lr * grad_hat/sqrt(v_hat+eps)
But objective scale invariant; log_a global drift matters not; normalization resets. Need choose lr. Since h dimension? We can scale gradient by S/P? To make step invariant: h/ (P/S?)? Let's nondimensionalize. C=P/S^2. h_i = ∂C/∂log a_i. For uniform, C=2. h magnitude maybe? We can scale step by C (dimensionless) / RMS? Adam:
```
m = 0; v=0; t=1
while time:
    c=fast_conv(a)
    w,P=compute_weights(c,mode,param)
    Qa=grad_a_weight(w,a)
    S=a.sum() (1)
    h = a*Qa - 2*P*a
    h -= h.mean()
    # optional: filter h to zero coordinates? 
    h_norm = np.linalg.norm(h)
    if h_norm<1e-12: stagnant++
    h = h / h_norm
    m = b1*m + (1-b1)*h
    v = b2*v + (1-b2)*h*h
    mh = m/(1-b1**t)
    vh = v/(1-b2**t)
    step = lr * mh / (sqrt(vh)+eps)
    loga = loga - step
    loga -= loga.max()
    a = exp(loga); normalize
```

Pseudo:
```
def adam_search(a, budget_s, seed, label, p_start=3.0, p_end=12.0):
    a=normalize(a); S target 1
    NFFT precompute? maybe not.
    loga=np.log(a+1e-300); center loga -= mean
    t0; deadline
    m=np.zeros; v=np.zeros; adam_t=0
    lr=0.05; best=a; best_score=fast_score(a)
    stagnant=0; fail=0
    while time<deadline:
       p = p_start + (p_end-p_start)*min(1, elapsed/budget)
       mode maybe 'lp'
       # optionally switch to lse last
       c=fast_conv(a); S=a.sum(); maxc=np.max(c)
       score=2*n*maxc/S^2
       w,P=weights(c,'lp',p) or lse
       Qa=grad_a_weights(w,a)
       h=a*Qa - 2*P*a
       h[h not finite]=0; h-=mean
       if norm small: break/stagnant
       h_norm = sqrt(mean(h*h)) maybe. Use RMS to scale.
       h = h / (h_norm + eps)
       adam_t +=1
       m=...
       step=lr*m_hat/(sqrt(v_hat)+1e-6)
       # limit step magnitude e.g. max abs 0.2
       step = np.clip(step, -0.3, 0.3)
       loga2=loga - step
       loga2 -= loga2.max()
       a2=np.exp(loga2)
       a2=normalize(a2)
       c2=fast_conv(a2); score2=...
       # accept if score2 <= score + 1e-8? If not, try smaller step half 3 times
       accept=False
       for trial in range(4):
          if score2 <= score * (1+tol) or score2 < best_score: accept True
          else step=step*0.7? But Adam step state? Could compute loga2 with smaller scale.
       if accept:
          loga=loga2; a=a2; c=c2; score=score2; if score<best: best...
          lr=min(0.2, lr*1.02); stagnant=0
       else:
          lr=max(0.005, lr*0.8)
          stagnant+=1
          if stagnant>50: random restart from best with perturbation; etc.
```

```
def _basis_forward(theta):
   y = _BASIS_B.dot(theta)
   y -= y.max()
   a = np.exp(y)
   s=a.sum(); if s<=0: uniform; else a/=s
   c=fast_convolution(a)
   w,P=compute_weights(c, _BASIS_MODE, _BASIS_PARAM)
   Qa=grad_a_weights(w,a)
   S=a.sum()
   C = P/(S*S)
   C_a = Qa/(S*S) - 2*P/(S**3) # derivative wrt a
   # gradient wrt theta_j: sum_i a_i C_a_i * (B_ij - sum a B_ij)
   B = _BASIS_B
   A = a[:, None]
   theta_grad = (B * (A * C_a[:,None]) @ np.ones?) More efficient:
       weighted = a * C_a
       # sum_i weighted_i B_ij = weighted @ B
       mean_B = a @ B  # shape K
```

Fast conv implementation:
```
def fast_convolution(a):
  n=a.size; N=fft_len(n); fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N); c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N); c=c[:2*n-1]; np.maximum(c,0,out=c); return c
```

Weights compute:
```
def compute_weights(c, mode, param):
  cpos=np.maximum(c,0.0)
  s=cpos.sum()
  if s<=0: return zeros,0
  if mode=='topk':
     K=int(param); K=max(1,min(K,c.size)); idx=np.argpartition(cpos,-K); top=idx[-K:]; w=zeros; w[top]=1/K; P=np.mean(cpos[top]); return w,P
  cmax=cpos.max()
  if cmax<=0: uniform
  if mode=='lse':
     beta=float(param)*cmax; beta=max(beta, cmax*1e-6)
     x=cpos/beta; x-=x.max(); expz=np.exp(x); sumz=expz.sum(); w=expz/sumz; P=float(np.dot(w,cpos))
  else: # lp
     p=float(param); if p<1: p=1
     cn=cpos/cmax
     if p==1: w=np.full_like(cn,1/cn.size); P=s/cn.size
     else:
        q=cn**(p-1)
        sq=q.sum()
        if sq<=0: uniform
        w=q/sq
        P=float(np.dot(w,cpos))
  return w,P
```
For lp p=1 uniform, gradient zero (since weights uniform P constant) -> h? Qa with uniform weights: Qa_i? c uniform weighted sum =S²/(2n-1)=constant, C constant, C_a=0. Our formula Qa_i? For uniform weights sum1, derivative of P wrt a is 2/S²? Let's check P=1/(2n-1) constant, Qa should be 2/(2n-1)? Actually P=∑_{i,t} p_i p_t/(2n-1)=1/(2n-1). Derivative =2/(2n-1), not zero. Wait P as weighted sum of c with uniform weights: c coefficients all weight 1/(2n-1); P=S²/(2n-1). Derivative wrt a_i =2/S²? Let's compute: ∂P/∂a_i =2/(2n-1). Our grad_a_weights formula: Qa_i=2∑_t w_{i+t}a_t with w uniform 1/(2n-1). For i fixed, sum over t a_t? i+t can be up to? w is defined only 0..2n-2; for all t, i+t in 0..i+n-1 ≤2n-2 yes, so sum=1. Qa=2/(2n-1). Good. Then C=P/S²=1/(2n-1). C_a=Qa/S² -2P/S³ =2/(2n-1)/S² -2 S²/(2n-1)/S³=0. Good. So h=0. Uniform weights useless. Use p>1.

Gradient A:
```
def grad_a_weights(w,a):
   n=a.size
   if n==1: return np.array([2*w[0]*a[0]])
   N=grad_fft_len(n)
   # zero pad w and rev a
   fw=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1],N)
   t=np.fft.irfft(fw*fa,N)
   return 2.0 * np.real(t[1:n+1])
```
Let's verify weights length 2n-1; t length N. For n=2, t[1:3]. Good. For n=1, if we used slice t[1:2] out. handle.

Implement `gradient_log_search` robust and reasonably fast. Let's write it fully.

```
def gradient_log_search(a, budget_s, seed=0, label="", sym=False, step_kind='adam', p_start=2.0, p_end=8.0):
    a = normalize_to_sum(np.asarray(a,float), 1.0)
    n=a.size
    if sym or n<=1:
       a=symmetrize(a,n)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    deadline = time.monotonic()+budget_s
    c=fast_convolution(a); score=fast_score_from_c(a,c)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=score
    loga=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); loga-=loga.mean()
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
    lr=0.08; max_step=0.25
    b1=0.9; b2=0.999; eps=1e-8
    stagnant=0; it=0; no_improve=0
    while time<deadline:
       it+=1
       if not finite a: reset
       c=fast_convolution(a); S=a.sum(); maxc=np.max(c); score=2n maxc/S²
       if not finite: reset
       elapsed_frac = min(1.0, time.monotonic()-t0)/(budget_s)
       p = p_start + (p_end-p_start)*smoothstep
       # choose mode: mostly lp; last 20% lse beta=0.25
       if elapsed_frac >0.8: mode='lse'; param=0.25
       else: mode='lp'; param=p
       w,P=compute_weights(c,mode,param)
       Qa=grad_a_weights(w,a)
       if not finite Qa: break
       Sa=a.sum(); P/= (Sa*Sa)? Wait calculate C = P/Sa². But weights independent of scale. For S=1. Use formulas.
       C=P/(Sa*Sa)
       Ca = Qa/(Sa*Sa) - 2.0*P/(Sa**3)
       h = a*Ca
       h -= h.mean()
       h = np.nan_to_num(h)
       rms = sqrt(mean(h*h)); if rms<1e-12: stagnant++; ...
       hu = h/rms
       if step_kind == 'adam':
           t +=1; m=...; v=...
           step = lr * mhat/(sqrt(vhat)+eps)
       elif step_kind == 'fixed':
           # use log ratio direction, no Adam
           # condition Qa=2P*a/S? For S=1. More generally, from Ca=0: Qa/S²=2P/S³ => Qa = 2P a/S.
           rhs = 2.0*P*a/Sa
           ratio = np.maximum(rhs,1e-15) / np.maximum(Qa,1e-15)
           stepdir = np.log(ratio)
           stepdir -= np.dot(a, stepdir)/Sa  # weighted center
           sd_rms = sqrt(mean(stepdir²))
           step = lr * stepdir / (sd_rms+eps)  # use lr as scale
           m=0;v=0? 
       step = np.clip(step, -max_step, max_step)
       # try scales
       accepted=False
       for scale in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125):
          loga2=loga - scale*step
          loga2 -= np.max(loga2)
          a2=np.exp(loga2)
          if sym: a2=symmetrize(a2,n)
          a2=normalize_to_sum(a2,1.0)
          if not finite: continue
          c2=fast_convolution(a2)
          score2=fast_score_from_c(a2,c2)
          if not finite: continue
          # accept if score2 <= score + 1e-10 or surrogate C2 < C? compute surrogate maybe expensive but can with same weights? skip
          if score2 <= score*(1.0 + 5e-6) + 1e-12:
             accepted=True; a=a2; c=c2; score=score2; loga=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); loga-=loga.mean(); break
       if accepted:
          if score < best_score:
             best=a.copy(); best_score=score; no_improve=0
          else: no_improve+=1
          lr = min(0.25, lr*1.02)
          stagnant=0
       else:
          lr = max(0.01, lr*0.75)
          stagnant +=1
          if stagnant > 80:
             # restart with perturbation
             a = smooth_perturbation(best, rng, 0.25)
             if sym: a=symmetrize(a,n)
             loga=...
             m=np.zeros; v=np.zeros; t=0; lr=0.1; stagnant=0; c=fast_convolution(a); score=fast_score...
          if no_improve > 2000:
             # small random kick
             loga += rng.normal(0,0.01,n); a=exp normalize; ...
```

**Pair swap refinement** function `pair_swap_polish(a,budget_s,seed,sym=False)`:
- Normalize. c,score current. Best.
- While time:
   Propose a new a:
     - If sym, choose pair symmetric? Could maintain full.
     - Choose method: random pair (i,j); δ = rng.uniform(-1,1)*min(a_i, a_j)*α maybe; or move towards zero: δ = -0.5*a_i, -0.1a_i etc.
     - Or choose i,j and set a_i' = sqrt? Sample from geometry: For pair, candidate δ uniform in [-a_j, a_i]. This explores. Use α to limit step around current: δ = rng.uniform(0,1)*0.2*(a_i-a_j) direction? Simpler: pick i,j, δ = rng.normal(0, 0.05*(a_i+a_j)); clip.
     - Apply, clip negative, normalize.
     - Evaluate fast score. If improve, accept.
   Also occasionally **merge small values**: pick smallest index, distribute its mass to neighbors or uniform.
But this may be slow random walk. Maybe skip; Adam enough. But pair swaps can fine-tune exact. We can include a short time after main search. Use 10% budget.

Could implement **coordinate descent by solving scalar** for one coordinate while scaling others? Let's try.
For coordinate i, keep relative ratios of others and sum. Parameter t = new a_i ∈ [0,1]. Others scaled by (1-t)/(1-a_i) if a_i<1. This reduces other coordinates equally, preserving their relative distribution. This can move mass in/out of a coordinate and compress others. Objective piecewise quadratic? We can minimize over t with grid maybe 20 points around current and plausible (0, best values). For each t, compute new a, c, score. Choose best t. Cycle random coordinates. This is like coordinate descent in probability simplex along rays. More systematic than pair.
Algorithm:
```
def coordinate_ray_polish(a,budget):
  for it in time:
    i = random or coordinate with highest/low? Maybe choose random.
    old=a.copy(); cur=a_i
    propose t values: 0, old*0.5, old*1.5 (≤1), maybe best_score values? Using current score M, t can't exceed sqrt(M)≈0.04 for n=512? old ~0.002. Try [0, 0.1,0.2,0.5,1,2,3,5,10]*old plus exact 0 and 1-eps.
    for t in candidates within [0,1]:
       a2=a.copy(); a2[i]=t; a2[others]*=(1-t)/(1-cur) if cur<1 else normalize
       normalize; sym if sym
       score=fast_score; choose min
    if best candidate improves current, accept; if improves global update.
```

Top-level `_basis_forward(theta)`:
```
global _BASIS...
if _BASIS_B is None: return uniform...
y = _BASIS_B.dot(theta)
# if y contains nonfinite: set 0
if not finite: ...
y = np.where(finite,y,0)
y -= y.max()
y = np.clip(y, -50, 50) # to avoid under/overflow; gradient slightly off but okay? Clipping can break L-BFGS if at bounds. Bounds keep y within maybe K*5=80, clip 50 okay rarely. 
a=np.exp(y)
s=a.sum()
if s<=0: uniform
a/=s
c=fast_convolution(a)
w,P=compute_weights(c,_BASIS_MODE,_BASIS_PARAM)
Qa=grad_a_weights(w,a)
S=s
C=P/(S*S)
Ca=Qa/(S*S) - 2.0*P/(S**3)
weighted=a*Ca
B=_BASIS_B
MB=a @ B
Wsum=float(weighted.sum())
grad=B.T @ weighted - Wsum * MB
# clean
grad=np.where(finite,grad,0)
_cache theta, a, val=C, grad
return a,C,weighted,grad
```
If using L-BFGS, objective C surrogate. If gradient bad, L-BFGS may fail. We can use `result.x` and final a.

```
   if _BASIS_LAST_THETA is not None and theta.shape==... and np.allclose(theta,_BASIS_LAST_THETA,rtol=1e-12,atol=1e-12): return _BASIS_LAST_VAL
   _,val,_,_=_basis_forward(theta)
   _BASIS_LAST_THETA=theta.copy(); _BASIS_LAST_VAL=val
```

Basis search function:
```
def basis_search(n, budget_s, parent, rng, max_k=16):
  if not HAS_SCIPY: return []
  results=[]
  t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+budget_s
  kinds=['cos_sym','cos_full','poly_sym']
  Ks=[8,12]
  for kind in kinds:
    for K in Ks:
       if time>deadline: break
       B=make_basis(n,kind,K); if B is None skip
       _BASIS_B=B; _BASIS_N=n; _BASIS_MODE='lp'; _BASIS_PARAM=3.0 # maybe 4
       B.shape K
       inits=[]
       inits.append(np.zeros(K))
       if kind in cos: inits.append([-2.0]+[0,...], [2.0]+...)
       if poly: inits.append([2.0]+..., [-2.0]+...)
       # random 3
       maxiter = max(30, min(200, int(max(1, (deadline-time.monotonic())/ (K+1)))) )? Better fixed 80.
       for idx,theta0 in enumerate(inits[:5]):
           if time>deadline - 10: break
           maxiter = 80 if K<=10 else 50
           try:
             res=_scipy_minimize(basis_value, theta0, jac=basis_jacobian, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=[(-4,4)]*K, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'maxls':40,'ftol':1e-10,'gtol':1e-6})
             # get a from res.x
             a, val, _, _ = _basis_forward(res.x)
             score=fast_score_array(a)
             if finite: results.append((score,a))
           except Exception: pass
  return results
```
Need top-level global assignment. Since `_basis_forward` uses `_BASIS_B`.

But using global cache with allclose can be expensive? K small.

Sanitize:
```
def sanitize_for_return(seq):
   a=np.asarray(seq,float)
   a=np.where(finite,a,0)
   a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<1e-12: return [1.0]*512
   a = a / s  # sum=1
   # If max >1000 (impossible after sum1), clip
   a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   if a.sum()<0.01: return [1.0]*512
   return [float(x) for x in a]
```
Should we rescale to sum=1? Evaluator scale invariant, but sum=1 avoids large/small. Good.

Potential issue: evaluate_sequence uses `np.convolve` on list; for sum=1 and n=1024, values ~0.001, conv max ~0.001, no underflow. Good.

**Fast score from c**:
```
def fast_score_from_c(a,c):
  n=a.size; S=a.sum(); if S<0.01: return inf
  maxc=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else inf
  return float(2*n*maxc/(S*S))
```
If maxc negative? c clipped nonneg.

Let's refine `gradient_log_search` for speed and stability.

Pseudo full:
```
def gradient_log_search(a, budget_s, seed=0, label="", sym=False, step_kind='adam', p_start=2.0, p_end=10.0):
    a = normalize_to_sum(np.array(a,float),1.0)
    if sym: a=symmetrize(a)
    n=a.size
    if budget_s <=0: return a, fast_score_array(a)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+budget_s
    c=fast_convolution(a); S=1; score=...
    best=a.copy(); best_score=score
    loga=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); loga-=loga.mean()
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t_adam=0
    lr=0.08; accept_tol=5e-6
    stagnant=0; no_improve=0
    t0=start; budget=max(1e-6,budget_s)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        # phase p
        frac = min(1.0, (time.monotonic()-t0)/budget)
        if step_kind == 'fixed':
             p = 3.0 + 7.0*frac # maybe
        else: p = p_start + (p_end-p_start)*frac
        mode = 'lse' if frac > 0.85 else 'lp'
        param = 0.25 if mode=='lse' else p
        c = fast_convolution(a)
        S = float(a.sum())
        if S <=0 or not finite: reset
        maxc = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else inf
        score = 2*n*maxc/(S*S)
        if not finite: reset
        w,P = compute_weights(c,mode,param)
        Qa = grad_a_weights(w,a)
        if not finite Qa: break
        Ca = Qa/(S*S) - 2.0*P/(S**3)
        h = a*Ca
        h = np.where(finite,h,0)
        h -= h.mean()
        if step_kind == 'fixed':
             rhs = 2.0*P*a/S
             ratio = np.maximum(rhs,1e-300) / np.maximum(Qa,1e-300)
             step = lr * np.log(ratio)
             step = np.where(finite,step,0)
             step -= float(np.dot(a, step)/S)
             # damp
             step = np.clip(step, -0.3, 0.3)
        else:
             rms = sqrt(np.mean(h*h))
             if rms < 1e-14:
                 stagnant += 100
                 if stagnant>50: break
                 continue
             hu = h/rms
             t_adam +=1
             m = 0.9*m +0.1*hu
             v = 0.999*v +0.001*hu*hu
             bh = m/(1-0.9**t_adam)
             bhv = v/(1-0.999**t_adam)
             step = lr * bh/(sqrt(bhv)+1e-6)
             step = np.clip(step, -0.25, 0.25)
        # try scales
        accepted=False
        for scale in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125):
           loga2 = loga - scale*step
           loga2 -= np.max(loga2)
           a2 = np.exp(loga2)
           if sym: a2=symmetrize(a2)
           a2=normalize_to_sum(a2,1.0)
           if not finite a2: continue
           c2=fast_convolution(a2)
           S2=float(a2.sum())
           maxc2=float(np.max(c2)) if c2.size else inf
           score2=2*n*maxc2/(S2*S2)
           if not finite: continue
           P2=float(np.dot(w, c2))  # using current weights; could be negative? c2 nonneg
           C2=P2/(S2*S2)
           C_current=P/(S*S)
           if score2 <= score*(1.0+accept_tol) or (C2 < C_current*(1.0-1e-5) and score2 <= score*1.002):
               accepted=True
               a=a2; c=c2; S=S2; score=score2
               loga=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); loga-=loga.mean()
               break
        if accepted:
           if score < best_score:
               best=a.copy(); best_score=score; no_improve=0
           else: no_improve +=1
           lr=min(0.25, lr*1.02)
           stagnant=0
        else:
           lr=max(0.008, lr*0.75)
           stagnant +=1
           if stagnant > 60:
               a=smooth_perturbation(best,rng,0.2)
               if sym: a=symmetrize(a)
               c=fast_convolution(a); S=a.sum(); score=...
               loga=...
               m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t_adam=0; lr=0.1; stagnant=0
        if no_improve > 3000:
             # annealing kick
             kick = rng.normal(0,0.02,n)
             loga += kick; loga-=loga.mean()
             a=np.exp(loga); if sym sym; a=normalize
             c=fast_convolution(a); S=a.sum(); score=...
             no_improve=0; lr=min(0.15, lr*1.5)
    return best, best_score
```

```
def symmetrize(a, target_sum=1.0):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    b=0.5*(a + a[::-1])
    return normalize_to_sum(b, target_sum)
```

```
def try_add_start(starts, a, n):
    try:
       arr=np.asarray(a,float)
       if arr.size != n: arr=upsample_sequence(arr, n)
       arr=normalize_to_sum(arr,1.0)
       if np.any(~finite): arr=np.nan_to_num(arr,0,0,0)
       sc=fast_score_array(arr)
       if finite: starts.append((sc,arr.copy()))
    except: pass
```

**Basis `make_basis`**: Need if n=1. For n=1, skip basis.
```
def make_basis(n, kind, K):
   if n<2 or K<1: return None
   t = -1.0 + 2.0*np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
   cols=[]
   if kind=='cos_sym':
      for m in range(1,K+1): cols.append(np.cos(np.pi*m*t))
   elif kind=='cos_full':
      m=1; c=0
      while c<K:
         cols.append(np.cos(np.pi*m*t)); c+=1
         if c<K: cols.append(np.sin(np.pi*m*t)); c+=1
         m+=1
   elif kind=='poly_sym':
      p=2; c=0
      while c<K: cols.append(np.power(t,p)); p+=2; c+=1
   elif kind=='poly_full':
      for p in range(1,K+1): cols.append(np.power(t,p))
   else: return None
   if not cols: return None
   B=np.column_stack(cols)
   B = np.nan_to_num(B,0.0,0.0,0.0)
   # remove constant
   B -= B.mean(axis=0)
   # scale each col by max abs
   for j in range(B.shape[1]):
      amax=float(np.max(np.abs(B[:,j])))
      if amax > 1e-12: B[:,j] /= amax
   # remove zero cols
   keep = np.array? To avoid list? use np.where
   good = np.max(np.abs(B), axis=0) > 1e-12
   B = B[:, good]
   if B.shape[1]==0: return None
   return B
```
No lambda. Good.

`basis_search` inits: For cos, first column cos(πt). For poly_sym, first t². Need know K after B maybe columns count. Use B.shape[1]. Inits:
```
def basis_initializers(B, kind, rng):
   K=B.shape[1]; inits=[]
   inits.append(np.zeros(K))
   if kind.startswith('cos'):
       for a in (-3.0,-2.0,-1.0,1.0,2.0,3.0): theta=np.zeros(K); theta[0]=a; inits.append(theta)
   else:
       for a in (-3.0,-2.0,-1.0,1.0,2.0,3.0): theta[0]=a; inits.append
   # random
   for i in range(3): inits.append(rng.normal(0,1,K)*0.7)
   return inits
```
Top-level.

But if B first column not cos? In `make_basis` cos first cos; poly first t². Good.

**Scipy not available**: `HAS_SCIPY` flag. If not, skip basis.

**`run_length`** detailed:
```
def run_length(n, budget_s, parent_seqs, seed, external_best):
    n=int(n); budget=max(0,float(budget_s))
    if budget <=2: return None, inf
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    deadline=time.monotonic()+budget
    starts=generate_starts(n, parent_seqs, rng, count=25)
    if external_best is not None: add_start_and_sym(starts, external_best, n)
    # basis
    basis_time = 0.22*budget if n<=2048 and budget>180 and HAS_SCIPY else 0
    if basis_time > 30:
        bres = basis_search(n, basis_time, parent_seqs, rng)
        for sc,a in bres: try_add_start(starts,a,n)
    # sort
    starts.sort(key=pool_key)
    best_seq=None; best_score=inf
    # also track best sym start
    best_sym_seq=None; best_sym_score=inf
    for sc,a in starts:
        if sc < best_score: best_score=sc; best_seq=a.copy()
        if sc < best_sym_score: pass # need sym score
    # compute sym scores maybe on fly:
    sym_score=[]
    for idx,(sc,a) in enumerate(starts):
        b=symmetrize(a,1.0); scb=fast_score_array(b)
        if scb < best_sym_score: best_sym_score=scb; best_sym_seq=b
    # local phases
    total = max(0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if total <=5: return best_seq,best_score
    phases=[]
    if total > 120:
       phases=[(True,'fixed',0.4),(False,'adam',0.3),(True,'adam',0.15),(False,'fixed',0.15)]
    elif total>60: phases=[(True,'fixed',0.5),(False,'adam',0.5)]
    else: phases=[(True,'fixed',1.0)]
    for phase_idx, (sym,kind,frac) in enumerate(phases):
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 5: break
       bphase = (deadline - time.monotonic()) * frac
       if bphase < 5: break
       if sym:
           start = best_sym_seq if best_sym_seq is not None else (best_seq if best_seq is not None else np.ones(n))
           # ensure sym start is sym score best? use best current if sym
           if best_seq is not None and symmetrize(best_seq,1).score < best_sym_score? update
       else:
           start = best_seq if best_seq is not None else np.ones(n)
       seq, score = gradient_log_search(start, bphase, seed=seed+phase_idx*101, label=..., sym=sym, step_kind=kind)
       if seq is not None and score < best_score:
           best_score=score; best_seq=seq.copy()
       # update sym best
       if sym:
          b=symmetrize(seq,1); scb=fast_score_array(b)
          if scb<best_sym_score: ...
       else:
          # also if seq full better sym? maybe
       add to starts? not needed
    return best_seq,best_score
```
In phases, local duration should not overlap: If we use `remaining * frac`, after first phase remaining reduced; the frac sum >1, could exceed. Better allocate fractions of initial total: `phase_budget = total_frac * frac`, but if first finishes early, next gets its original. Use `time_each = total_frac * frac` and subtract minimum. Simpler: compute `phase_times = [total*frac for ...]`, then loop with `bphase = min(phase_times[i], deadline - time.monotonic() - 5)`. This allocates fixed.
```
total = deadline - now
phases = [...]
phase_times = [total*f for ...]
for i,(sym,kind),pt in zip...
```
Good.
Update best_sym after each phase: if best_seq sym improved.

In `fft_len`: 
```
def fft_len(n):
    key=int(n)
    N=_FFT_CACHE.get(key)
    if N is None:
       L=2*key-1
       N=1 << (L-1).bit_length()
       _FFT_CACHE[key]=N
    return N
```
If L=1, (0).bit_length()=0, 1<<0=1. Good.
`grad_fft_len`: M=3*n-2; N=1<<((M-1).bit_length()). For n=1 M=1 N=1.

`fast_convolution`:
```
def fast_convolution(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.empty(0)
    N=fft_len(n)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)
    L=2*n-1
    c=c[:L].copy()
    np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)
    return c
```
Why copy? c slice view of full; max out okay but maybe not owner? `c = np.ascontiguousarray(c)`. Use `c = np.ascontiguousarray(c[:L])`.

`fast_score_array`: if n==0 inf. `S=float(np.sum(a))`; if S<0.01 inf. `c=fast_convolution(a); maxc=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else inf; return float(2*n*maxc/(S*S))`.

`normalize_to_sum`:
```
def normalize_to_sum(a, target=1.0):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    if a.size==0: return np.ones(max(1,int(target))) / max(1,int(target))? But target sum length needed. If empty return None? We shouldn't call empty. For safety: if a.size==0: return np.array([1.0],float)
    a=np.where(finite,a,0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=1e-300:
       a.fill(1.0/a.size)
       s=a.size
    a = a * (target/s)
    a=np.minimum(a, _MAXELL)
    # after clip, rescale up to 3 times
    for _ in range(3):
       s2=float(a.sum())
       if s2<=1e-300: a.fill(...); break
       a = a * (target/s2)
       a=np.minimum(a,_MAXELL)
    return a
```
After clip to 1000, if target=1 no clip. If target=n large maybe not. We use target1.

`upsample_sequence`:
```
def upsample_sequence(seq, n):
    seq=np.asarray(seq,float)
    m=seq.size
    n=int(n)
    if m==0: return normalize_to_sum(np.ones(n),1.0)
    if m==n: return normalize_to_sum(seq,1.0)
    xold=np.linspace(0,1,m); xnew=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    out=np.interp(xnew,xold,seq)
    return normalize_to_sum(out,1.0)
```

`symmetrize`:
```
def symmetrize(a, target_sum=1.0):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   if a.size==0: return normalize_to_sum(np.ones(1),target_sum)
   b=0.5*(a + a[::-1])
   return normalize_to_sum(b, target_sum)
```

`zero_small`:
```
def zero_small(a, thresh):
   a=np.array(a,float,copy=True)
   a[a<thresh]=0
   return normalize_to_sum(a,1.0)
```

```
def try_add_start(starts, a, n):
   try:
     arr=np.asarray(a,float)
     if arr.size != n:
        arr=upsample_sequence(arr,n)
     else:
        arr=normalize_to_sum(arr,1.0)
     if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)): arr=np.nan_to_num(arr,0.0,0.0,0.0)
     sc=fast_score_array(arr)
     if np.isfinite(sc):
        starts.append((sc, arr.copy()))
   except Exception:
     pass
```

```
def add_start_and_sym(starts, a, n):
    try_add_start(starts,a,n)
    try:
       arr=np.asarray(a,float)
       if arr.size != n: arr=upsample_sequence(arr,n)
       else: arr=normalize_to_sum(arr,1.0)
       b=symmetrize(arr,1.0)
       try_add_start(starts,b,n)
    except Exception: pass
```

`make_harmonic_sequence` maybe top-level for random starts:
```
def make_harmonic_sequence(n, rng):
    n=int(n); if n<2: return normalize_to_sum(np.ones(n),1.0)
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n); y=np.zeros(n)
    # low freq cos/sin
    for m in range(1,7):
       amp=rng.uniform(-1,1)/(m+0.5)
       ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       y += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*x+ph)
       if rng.random()<0.5: y += amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*x+ph)
    # add sine endpoint bump
    y += rng.uniform(-0.5,0.5)*np.sin(np.pi*x)
    y -= y.max()
    a=np.exp(y)
    return normalize_to_sum(a,1.0)
```
Could create zeros if exp underflow? okay.

`make_two_block` maybe:

```
def make_two_block(n, frac, ratio, rng?) deterministic:
   m=int(frac*n); denom=m*ratio+n-m; b=n/denom; a=...; return normalize
```
But U shapes more important. Include two-block for diversity.

`generate_starts`:
```
def generate_starts(n, parents, rng, count=30):
   starts=[]
   n=int(n)
   if n<=0: return []
   add_start_and_sym(starts, np.ones(n), n)
   if parents:
      for p in parents[:5]: add_start_and_sym(starts,p,n)
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   t=2*x-1
   at=np.abs(t)
   # U shapes
   for alpha in np.linspace(0.0,3.0,25):
       a=1.0 + alpha*(2.0*t*t - 1.0)
       add_start_and_sym(starts, a, n)
   for alpha in [0.5,1.0,1.5,2.0,3.0,4.0]:
       a=np.maximum(0.0, alpha - np.cos(np.pi*t))
       add_start_and_sym(starts, a, n)
   for p in [0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0]:
       a=np.power(at, p) + 0.05
       add_start_and_sym(starts, a, n)
       a=np.power(np.maximum(0.0, 2.0*at - 1.0), p) + 0.01
       add_start_and_sym(starts, a, n)
   # caps for diversity
   for alpha in [0.5,1.0,2.0,3.0]:
       a=1.0 + alpha*(1.0 - 4.0*x*(1.0-x)) # endpoint high? x(1-x) center max -> endpoints 1+alpha, center 1-alpha
       add_start_and_sym(starts, a, n)
   # two blocks
   for frac in [0.2,0.3,0.4]:
      for ratio in [0.5,1.5,3.0]:
          add_start_and_sym(starts, make_two_block(n,frac,ratio), n)
   # random
   for _ in range(count):
       kind=rng.random()
       if kind<0.5:
           a=make_harmonic_sequence(n,rng)
       else:
           a=np.ones(n)
           k=int(rng.integers(max(1,n//100), max(2,n//5+1)))
           idx=int(rng.integers(0,n))
           a[idx:idx+%=np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.5,k))
           a[:]=np.maximum(a,0)
           a[~np.isfinite(a)]=1
       add_start_and_sym(starts,a,n)
   # limit and sort
   if len(starts)>120: 
       starts.sort(key=pool_key)
       starts=starts[:120]
   starts.sort(key=pool_key)
   return starts
```
`make_two_block` top-level:
```
def make_two_block(n, frac, ratio):
    n=int(n); m=int(round(frac*n)); if m<1: m=1; if m>=n: m=n-1
    denominator=m*ratio+(n-m)
    B=n/denominator; A=ratio*B
    a=np.empty(n); a[:m]=A; a[m:]=B
    return normalize_to_sum(a,1.0)
```
For random block, modulo end: `stop=min(n, idx+k)`, pad if shorter. Simpler:
```
k=...; start=...; a[start:start+k]=np.exp(...); a=np.nan_to_num(a)
```
If start+k>n, k shorter? Use stop.

`smooth_perturbation`:
```
def smooth_perturbation(a, rng, strength=0.25):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).copy()
   n=a.size
   if n>2:
      x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
      z=np.zeros(n)
      for m in (1,2,3):
         amp=strength/(m+0.5)
         ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
         z += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*x+ph)
      z += rng.uniform(-strength*0.5, strength*0.5)*np.cos(np.pi*(2*x-1))
      a *= np.exp(z)
   if n>8:
      for _ in range(max(1,n//256)):
         idx=int(rng.integers(0,n))
         a[idx] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, strength*0.5))
   a=np.where(finite,a,0)
   a=np.maximum(a, 1e-12/n if n>0 else 0)
   return normalize_to_sum(a,1.0)
```

`final_select`:
```
def final_select(pool, deadline):
   pool.sort(key=pool_key)
   best_seq=None; best_score=inf
   # top candidates transform
   exact_calls=0
   max_calls=10
   for idx,(fastsc,a) in enumerate(pool[:6]):
       if exact_calls>=max_calls: break
       trans=[a]
       if idx<3:
          trans.append(symmetrize(a,1.0))
          trans.append(zero_small(a,1e-7))
       # maybe sym zero if idx<2
       for b in trans:
          if exact_calls>=max_calls or time.monotonic()>deadline-2: break
          try:
             lst=sanitize_for_return(b)
             ex=evaluate_sequence(lst)
             exact_calls+=1
             if np.isfinite(ex) and ex<best_score:
                best_score=float(ex); best_seq=list(lst) # or b
          except Exception: pass
   if best_seq is None:
      # fallback uniform 512
      a=np.ones(512)
      lst=sanitize_for_return(a)
      try: ex=evaluate_sequence(lst)
      except: ex=inf
      if finite: best_score=ex
      else: best_score=2.0
      best_seq=lst
   return best_seq, best_score
```

```
def _basis_forward(theta):
    global _BASIS_LAST_THETA, _BASIS_LAST_VAL, _BASIS_LAST_GRAD
    if _BASIS_B is None:
       n=int(_BASIS_N) if _BASIS_N>0 else 1
       a=normalize_to_sum(np.ones(n),1.0)
       val=fast_score_array(a)
       _BASIS_LAST_THETA=theta.copy(); _BASIS_LAST_VAL=val; _BASIS_LAST_GRAD=np.zeros(theta.size)
       return a,val,np.zeros(theta.size)
    B=_BASIS_B
    y=B.dot(theta)
    y=np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)
    ymax=float(np.max(y))
    y=y - ymax
    y=np.clip(y, -60.0, 0.0)
    a=np.exp(y)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
       a=np.ones(B.shape[0]); s=float(a.size)
    a=a/(s)
    c=fast_convolution(a)
    w,P=compute_weights(c,_BASIS_MODE,_BASIS_PARAM)
    Qa=grad_a_weights(w,a)
    S=float(a.sum())
    val = float(P/(S*S))
    Ca = Qa/(S*S) - (2.0*P)/(S*S*S)
    Ca = np.where(np.isfinite(Ca), Ca, 0.0)
    weighted = a*Ca
    MB = a.dot(B)
    Wsum = float(weighted.sum())
    grad = B.T.dot(weighted) - Wsum*MB
    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
    _BASIS_LAST_THETA=theta.copy(); _BASIS_LAST_VAL=val; _BASIS_LAST_GRAD=grad
    return a,val,grad
```
`B.T.dot(weighted)` works (K,n)@(n,)=K. Good.

```
        return float(_BASIS_LAST_VAL)
    _, val, _ = _basis_forward(theta)
```

```
def compute_weights(c, mode, param):
   cpos=np.asarray(c,float)
   cpos=np.where(cpos>0, cpos, 0.0)
   L=cpos.size
   if L==0: return np.empty(0),0.0
   s=float(cpos.sum())
   if s <=0:
      w=np.full(L,1.0/L); return w,0.0
   if mode == 'topk':
      K=int(param)
      if K<=1: idx=np.argmax(cpos); w=zeros; w[idx]=1; P=cpos[idx]
      else:
         K=min(K,L); idx=np.argpartition(cpos, -K)[-K:]; w=zeros; w[idx]=1.0/K; P=float(np.mean(cpos[idx]))
      return w,P
   cmax=float(np.max(cpos))
   if cmax<=0: uniform
   if mode == 'lse': beta=...; x=cpos/beta; x-=x.max(); ez=np.exp(x); sz=ez.sum(); w=ez/sz; P=float(np.dot(w,cpos)); return
   # lp
   p=float(param)
   if p <= 1.0:
      w=np.full(L,1.0/L); P=s/L; return
   cn=cpos/cmax
   if p > 20: p=20
   q=np.power(cn, p-1.0)
   sq=float(q.sum())
   if sq <=0: ...
   w=q/sq; P=float(np.dot(w,cpos)); return
```

```python
"""
Searches for non-negative step-function heights minimizing

    2 * n * max(convolve(a, a)) / (sum(a)**2)

The sequence is treated as a probability vector (scale invariant objective).
The search combines:
  - diverse deterministic / random initializers (end-heavy U shapes, Fourier shapes),
  - low-dimensional L-BFGS-B refinement in a cosine / polynomial basis,
  - full-dimensional smoothed mirror/Adam local search in log-probability space,
  - extramar ortam symmetric variants,
  - exact final validation with the provided evaluator.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _scipy_minimize
    _HAS_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    _HAS_SCIPY = False


_EPS = 1e-12
_MAXELL = 1000.0
_FFT_CACHE = {}
_GRAD_FFT_CACHE = {}

 Basis globals for L-BFGS refinement ----------------

_BASIS_B = None
_BASIS_N = 0
_BASIS_MODE = "lp"
_BASIS_PARAM = 3.0
_BASIS_LAST_THETA = None
_BASIS_LAST_VAL = None
_BASIS_LAST_GRAD = None


def pool_key(pair):
    return pair[0]


def normalize_to_sum(a, target_sum=1.0):
    """Clip to finite non-negative values and rescale to target sum."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)

    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)

    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        a.fill(1.0 / float(a.size))
        s = float(a.size)

    a = a * (float(target_sum) / s)
    a = np.minimum(a, _MAXELL)

    for _ in range(3):
        s2 = float(a.sum())
        if s2 <= 1e-300:
            a.fill(float(target_sum) / float(a.size))
            break
        a = a * (float(target_sum) / s2)
        a = np.minimum(a, _MAXELL)

    return a


def upsample_sequence(seq, n):
    """Linearly interpolate a probability-like sequence to length n and renormalize."""
    seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
    m = int(seq.size)
    n = int(n)

    if n <= 0:
        n = 1

    if m == 0:
        return normalize_to_sum(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), 1.0)
    if m == n:
        return normalize_to_sum(seq, 1.0)

    xold = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m, dtype=np.float64)
    xnew = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    out = np.interp(xnew, xold, seq)
    return normalize_to_sum(out, 1.0)


def symmetrize(a, target_sum=1.0):
    """Average with reversal and renormalize."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return normalize_to_sum(np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64), target_sum)
    b = 0.5 * (a + a[::-1])
    return normalize_to_sum(b, target_sum)


def zero_small(a, thresh):
    """Set tiny entries to zero and renormalize."""
    a = np.array(a, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a[a < thresh] = 0.0
    return normalize_to_sum(a, 1.0)


def fft_len(n):
    n = int(n)
    key = n
    out = _FFT_CACHE.get(key)
    if out is None:
        L = 2 * key - 1
        out = 1 << (L - 1).bit_length()
        _FFT_CACHE[key] = out
    return out


def grad_fft_len(n):
    n = int(n)
    key = n
    out = _GRAD_FFT_CACHE.get(key)
    if out is None:
        L = 3 * key - 2
        out = 1 << (L - 1).bit_length()
        _GRAD_FFT_CACHE[key] = out
    return out


def fast_convolution(a):
    """FFT-based linear convolution a * a, truncated to length 2n-1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    N = fft_len(n)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    c = np.ascontiguousarray(c[:L])
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def fast_score_from_c(a, c):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    maxc = float(np.max(c))
    return float(2.0 * n * maxc / (S * S))


def fast_score_array(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    c = fast_convolution(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    maxc = float(np.max(c))
    return float(2.0 * a.size * maxc / (S * S))


def try_add_start(starts, a, n):
    """Normalize/uplength and append a candidate if finite."""
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        n = int(n)
        if n <= 0:
            return
        if arr.size != n:
            arr = upsample_sequence(arr, n)
        else:
            arr = normalize_to_sum(arr, 1.0)

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
            arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0)

        sc = fast_score_array(arr)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            starts.append((sc, arr.copy()))
    except Exception:
        pass


def add_start_and_sym(starts, a, n):
    """Add original and symmetrized versions of a candidate."""
    try_add_start(starts, a, n)
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        n = int(n)
        if n <= 0:
            return
        if arr.size != n:
            arr = upsample_sequence(arr, n)
        else:
            arr = normalize_to_sum(arr, 1.0)
        b = symmetrize(arr, 1.0)
        try_add_start(starts, b, n)
    except Exception:
        pass


def make_two_block(n, frac, ratio):
    """Deterministic two-block candidate."""
    n = int(n)
    if n < 2:
        return normalize_to_sum(np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64), 1.0)

    m = int(round(frac * n))
    if m < 1:
        m = 1
    if m >= n:
        m = n - 1

    denom = m * float(ratio) + float(n - m)
    if denom <= 0.0:
        B = 1.0
    else:
        B = float(n) / denom
    A = float(ratio) * B

    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[:m] = A
    a[m:] = B
    return normalize_to_sum(a, 1.0)


def make_harmonic_sequence(n, rng):
    """Random low-frequency smooth candidate, exponentiated."""
    n = int(n)
    if n < 2:
        return normalize_to_sum(np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64), 1.0)

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    for m in range(1, 7):
        amp = rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0) / (m + 0.5)
        ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        z += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * x + ph)
        if rng.random() < 0.7:
            z += amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * m * x + ph)

    z += rng.uniform(-0.6, 0.6) * np.sin(np.pi * x)
    z -= float(np.max(z))
    a = np.exp(z)
    return normalize_to_sum(a, 1.0)


def generate_starts(n, parents, rng, count=28):
    """Diverse starts for a given length."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return []

    starts = []

    add_start_and_sym(starts, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), n)

    if parents:
        for p in parents[:4]:
            add_start_and_sym(starts, p, n)

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 2.0 * x - 1.0
    at = np.abs(t)

    # Primary family: U-shaped shapes, especially high endpoints / low center.
    n_alpha = 25 if n <= 2048 else 15
    for alpha in np.linspace(0.0, 3.0, n_alpha, dtype=np.float64):
        a = 1.0 + alpha * (2.0 * t * t - 1.0)
        add_start_and_sym(starts, a, n)

    for alpha in (0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0):
        a = np.maximum(0.0, alpha - np.cos(np.pi * t))
        add_start_and_sym(starts, a, n)

    for p in (0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
        a = np.power(at, p) + 0.05
        add_start_and_sym(starts, a, n)
        a = np.power(np.maximum(0.0, 2.0 * at - 1.0), p) + 0.01
        add_start_and_sym(starts, a, n)

    # Some broader shapes / caps for escape.
    for alpha in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0):
        a = 1.0 + alpha * (1.0 - 4.0 * x * (1.0 - x))
        add_start_and_sym(starts, a, n)

    # Simple two-block shapes.
    for frac in (0.2, 0.3, 0.4):
        for ratio in (0.5, 1.5, 3.0):
            add_start_and_sym(starts, make_two_block(n, frac, ratio), n)

    # Random smooth / local patch candidates.
    count = max(4, int(count))
    for _ in range(count):
        if rng.random() < 0.55:
            a = make_harmonic_sequence(n, rng)
        else:
            a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
            if n > 4:
                k = int(rng.integers(max(1, n // 100), max(2, n // 5 + 1)))
                k = min(k, n)
                st = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - k + 1)))
                a[st:st + k] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.45, k))
            a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 1.0)
            a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
        add_start_and_sym(starts, a, n)

    # Keep a manageable pool.
    max_starts = 120 if n <= 1024 else 70
    if len(starts) > max_starts:
        starts.sort(key=pool_key)
        starts = starts[:max_starts]

    starts.sort(key=pool_key)
    return starts


def smooth_perturbation(a, rng, strength=0.25):
    """Smooth multiplicative perturbation for restarts."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return a

    if n > 2:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
        z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for m in (1, 2, 3):
            amp = strength / (float(m) + 0.5)
            ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
            z += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * x + ph)
        z += rng.uniform(-0.5 * strength, 0.5 * strength) * np.cos(np.pi * (2.0 * x - 1.0))
        a *= np.exp(z)

    if n > 8:
        for _ in range(max(1, n // 256)):
            idx = int(rng.integers(0, n))
            a[idx] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.5 * strength))

    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12 / float(n) if n > 0 else 0.0)
    return normalize_to_sum(a, 1.0)


def compute_weights(c, mode, param):
    """
    Return weights w (summing to 1) and P = sum w_k c_k.
    mode: 'lp', 'lse', 'topk'
    """
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    L = int(c.size)

    if L == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64), 0.0

    s = float(c.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        w = np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)
        return w, 0.0

    if mode == "topk":
        K = int(param)
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        if K <= 1:
            idx = int(np.argmax(c))
            w[idx] = 1.0
            P = float(c[idx])
        else:
            K = min(K, L)
            idx = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
            w[idx] = 1.0 / float(K)
            P = float(np.mean(c[idx]))
        return w, float(P)

    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if cmax <= 0.0:
        w = np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)
        return w, 0.0

    if mode == "lse":
        beta = float(param)
        if not np.isfinite(beta) or beta <= 0.0:
            beta = 0.25
        beta = max(beta * cmax, 1e-300)
        z = c / beta
        z -= float(np.max(z))
        ez = np.exp(z)
        sz = float(ez.sum())
        if sz <= 0.0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(c))
            w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            w[idx] = 1.0
            return w, float(c[idx])
        w = ez / sz
        P = float(np.dot(w, c))
        return w, P

    # default: Lp weights, p = param
    p = float(param)
    if not np.isfinite(p) or p <= 1.0:
        w = np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)
        return w, float(s / float(L))
    if p > 20.0:
        p = 20.0

    cn = c / cmax
    q = np.power(cn, p - 1.0)
    sq = float(q.sum())
    if sq <= 0.0:
        idx = int(np.argmax(c))
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        w[idx] = 1.0
        return w, float(c[idx])

    w = q / sq
    P = float(np.dot(w, c))
    return w, float(P)


def grad_a_weights(w, a):
    """
    Gradient of P(a) = sum_k w_k (a*a)_k with respect to a.

    If w_k is weight for convolution index k, then
    dP/da_i = 2 * sum_j a_j w_{i+j}, where valid indices are handled by padding.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    L = int(w.size)

    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if L == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    if n == 1:
        return np.array([2.0 * float(w[0]) * float(a[0])], dtype=np.float64)

    # Need full convolution of w (len 2n-1) and reversed a (len n).
    # Desired slice starts at index n-1, length n.
    target_len = 3 * n - 2
    N = grad_fft_len(n)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
    t = np.fft.irfft(fw * fa, N)
    gt = 2.0 * t[n - 1: 2 * n - 1]
    gt = np.ascontiguousarray(gt)
    np.nan_to_num(gt, copy=False, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return gt


def add_candidate_pool(pool, seq, score):
    """Add a sequence to a small pool sorted by fast score."""
    try:
        a = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
        if a.size == 0:
            return
        a = normalize_to_sum(a, 1.0)
        sc = fast_score_array(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            pool.append((float(sc), a.copy()))
            pool.sort(key=pool_key)
            if len(pool) > 24:
                pool.pop()
    except Exception:
        pass


def basis_initializers(B, kind, rng):
    """Return a list of initial parameter vectors for basis optimization."""
    K = int(B.shape[1])
    inits = []
    inits.append(np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64))

    if kind.startswith("cos"):
        for val in (-3.0, -2.0, -1.0, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0):
            th = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
            th[0] = val
            inits.append(th)
    else:
        for val in (-3.0, -2.0, -1.0, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0):
            th = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
            th[0] = val
            inits.append(th)

    for _ in range(3):
        inits.append(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, K) * 0.7)

    return inits


def make_basis(n, kind, K):
    """Bounded Fourier / polynomial basis, with columns scaled to max-abs 1."""
    n = int(n)
    K = int(K)
    if n < 2 or K < 1:
        return None

    t = -1.0 + 2.0 * np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    cols = []

    if kind == "cos_sym":
        for m in range(1, K + 1):
            cols.append(np.cos(np.pi * m * t))
    elif kind == "cos_full":
        m = 1
        c = 0
        while c < K and m <= 20:
            cols.append(np.cos(np.pi * m * t))
            c += 1
            if c < K:
                cols.append(np.sin(np.pi * m * t))
                c += 1
            m += 1
    elif kind == "poly_sym":
        p = 2
        c = 0
        while c < K and p <= 30:
            cols.append(np.power(t, p))
            p += 2
            c += 1
    elif kind == "poly_full":
        for p in range(1, K + 1):
            cols.append(np.power(t, p))
    else:
        return None

    if not cols:
        return None

    B = np.column_stack(cols).astype(np.float64, copy=True)
    B = np.nan_to_num(B, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0)

    # Remove constants to make parameters meaningful and reduce scale invariance flats.
    B = B - B.mean(axis=0)

    for j in range(B.shape[1]):
        amax = float(np.max(np.abs(B[:, j])))
        if amax > 1e-12:
            B[:, j] /= amax

    good = np.max(np.abs(B), axis=0) > 1e-12
    B = B[:, good]
    if B.size == 0 or B.shape[1] == 0:
        return None
    return B


def _basis_forward(theta):
    """
    Forward pass for basis parameterization.
    Returns (a, surrogate_value, gradient).
    """
    global _BASIS_LAST_THETA, _BASIS_LAST_VAL, _BASIS_LAST_GRAD

    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).ravel()

    if _BASIS_B is None:
        n = int(_BASIS_N) if _BASIS_N > 0 else 1
        a = normalize_to_sum(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), 1.0)
        val = fast_score_array(a)
        _BASIS_LAST_THETA = theta.copy()
        _BASIS_LAST_VAL = float(val)
        _BASIS_LAST_GRAD = np.zeros(theta.size, dtype=np.float64)
        return a, float(val), _BASIS_LAST_GRAD

    B = _BASIS_B
    y = B.dot(theta)
    y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)

    ymax = float(np.max(y))
    y = y - ymax
    y = np.clip(y, -60.0, 0.0)

    a = np.exp(y)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones(B.shape[0], dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(a.size)
    a = a / s

    c = fast_convolution(a)
    w, P = compute_weights(c, _BASIS_MODE, _BASIS_PARAM)
    Qa = grad_a_weights(w, a)

    S = float(a.sum())
    val = float(P / (S * S))

    Ca = Qa / (S * S) - (2.0 * P) / (S * S * S)
    Ca = np.where(np.isfinite(Ca), Ca, 0.0)

    weighted = a * Ca
    MB = a.dot(B)
    Wsum = float(weighted.sum())

    grad = B.T.dot(weighted) - Wsum * MB
    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)

    _BASIS_LAST_THETA = theta.copy()
    _BASIS_LAST_VAL = float(val)
    _BASIS_LAST_GRAD = grad
    return a, float(val), grad


def basis_value(theta):
    global _BASIS_LAST_THETA, _BASIS_LAST_VAL
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if (
        _BASIS_LAST_THETA is not None
        and theta.size == _BASIS_LAST_THETA.size
        and np.allclose(theta, _BASIS_LAST_THETA, rtol=1e-12, atol=1e-12)
    ):
        return float(_BASIS_LAST_VAL)
    _, val, _ = _basis_forward(theta)
    return float(val)


def basis_jacobian(theta):
    global _BASIS_LAST_THETA, _BASIS_LAST_GRAD
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if (
        _BASIS_LAST_THETA is not None
        and theta.size == _BASIS_LAST_THETA.size
        and np.allclose(theta, _BASIS_LAST_THETA, rtol=1e-12, atol=1e-12)
    ):
        return _BASIS_LAST_GRAD
    _, _, grad = _basis_forward(theta)
    return grad


def basis_search(n, budget_s, parents, rng):
    """Run several low-dimensional basis optimizations; return candidate list (score, a)."""
    global _BASIS_B, _BASIS_N, _BASIS_MODE, _BASIS_PARAM, _BASIS_LAST_THETA

    if not _HAS_SCIPY:
        return []

    n = int(n)
    budget_s = max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    if n < 8 or budget_s < 30.0:
        return []

    out = []
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + budget_s

    if n <= 1024:
        kinds = ["cos_sym", "cos_full", "poly_sym"]
        Ks = [8, 12]
        modes = [("lp", 3.0), ("lp", 6.0), ("lse", 0.5)]
        max_inits = 7
    else:
        kinds = ["cos_sym"]
        Ks = [8]
        modes = [("lp", 4.0)]
        max_inits = 5

    for mode, param in modes:
        for kind in kinds:
            for K in Ks:
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 10.0:
                    break

                B = make_basis(n, kind, K)
                if B is None:
                    continue

                _BASIS_B = B
                _BASIS_N = n
                _BASIS_MODE = mode
                _BASIS_PARAM = float(param)
                _BASIS_LAST_THETA = None

                inits = basis_initializers(B, kind, rng)
                Kuse = int(B.shape[1])
                bounds = [(-4.0, 4.0)] * Kuse
                maxiter = 70 if n <= 1024 else 50

                for inits_i in range(min(max_inits, len(inits))):
                    if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 5.0:
                        break

                    theta0 = np.asarray(inits[inits_i], dtype=np.float64).ravel()
                    if theta0.size != Kuse or not np.all(np.isfinite(theta0)):
                        continue

                    try:
                        res = _scipy_minimize(
                            basis_value,
                            theta0,
                            jac=basis_jacobian,
                            method="L-BFGS-B",
                            bounds=bounds,
                            options={"maxiter": maxiter, "maxls": 40, "ftol": 1e-9, "gtol": 1e-6},
                        )
                        theta_best = getattr(res, "x", theta0)
                        if theta_best is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(theta_best)):
                            theta_best = theta0

                        a, _, _ = _basis_forward(np.asarray(theta_best, dtype=np.float64).ravel())
                        score = fast_score_array(a)
                        if np.isfinite(score):
                            out.append((float(score), np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()))
                    except Exception:
                        pass

    return out


def gradient_log_search(a, budget_s, seed=0, sym=False, step_kind="adam",
                        p_start=2.0, p_end=10.0):
    """
    Local search in log-probability space.

    step_kind:
      - 'adam': Adam on the log-derivative of a smooth surrogate.
      - 'fixed': multiplicative fixed-point-like steps toward Qa = 2P a/S.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a = normalize_to_sum(a, 1.0)
    if sym:
        a = symmetrize(a, 1.0)

    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return a, float("inf")
    if n == 1:
        return a, 2.0

    budget_s = max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    if budget_s <= 0.0:
        return a, fast_score_array(a)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + budget_s

    c = fast_convolution(a)
    S = float(a.sum())
    maxc = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    score = float(2.0 * n * maxc / (S * S)) if S > 0 else float("inf")

    best = a.copy()
    best_score = score
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best = normalize_to_sum(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), 1.0)
        if sym:
            best = symmetrize(best, 1.0)
        c = fast_convolution(best)
        S = float(best.sum())
        maxc = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        best_score = float(2.0 * n * maxc / (S * S))
        a = best.copy()
        score = best_score

    def _logarray(arr):
        return np.log(np.maximum(arr, 1e-300))

    loga = _logarray(a)
    loga -= float(np.mean(loga))

    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t_adam = 0

    if step_kind == "fixed":
        lr = 0.05
        max_step = 0.30
    else:
        lr = 0.08
        max_step = 0.25

    b1 = 0.90
    b2 = 0.999
    eps_adam = 1e-7
    accept_tol = 5e-6

    stagnant = 0
    no_improve = 0
    budget_for_frac = max(1e-6, budget_s)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)) or float(a.sum()) <= 0.0:
            a = best.copy()
            c = fast_convolution(a)
            S = float(a.sum())
            score = best_score
            loga = _logarray(a)
            loga -= float(np.mean(loga))
            m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            stagnant = 0
            continue

        frac = min(1.0, max(0.0, (time.monotonic() - start) / budget_for_frac))

        if step_kind == "fixed":
            p = 3.0 + 6.0 * frac
            mode = "lp"
            param = p
        else:
            p = p_start + (p_end - p_start) * frac
            if frac > 0.82:
                mode = "lse"
                param = 0.25
            else:
                mode = "lp"
                param = p

        c = fast_convolution(a)
        S = float(a.sum())
        if S <= 0.0:
            break

        maxc = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        score = float(2.0 * n * maxc / (S * S))
        if not np.isfinite(score):
            a = best.copy()
            c = fast_convolution(a)
            S = float(a.sum())
            score = best_score
            loga = _logarray(a)
            loga -= float(np.mean(loga))
            m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            stagnant = 0
            continue

        w, P = compute_weights(c, mode, param)
        if P <= 0.0:
            break

        Qa = grad_a_weights(w, a)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(Qa)):
            break

        # Ca = d/d a of surrogate C = P/S^2.
        Ca = Qa / (S * S) - (2.0 * P) / (S * S * S)
        h = a * Ca
        h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
        h -= float(np.mean(h))

        if step_kind == "fixed":
            rhs = 2.0 * P * a / S
            ratio = np.maximum(rhs, 1e-300) / np.maximum(Qa, 1e-300)
            step = lr * np.log(ratio)
            step = np.where(np.isfinite(step), step, 0.0)
            step -= float(np.dot(a, step) / S)
            step = np.clip(step, -max_step, max_step)
        else:
            rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(h * h)))
            if rms < 1e-14:
                stagnant += 100
                if stagnant > 60:
                    a = smooth_perturbation(best, rng, 0.18)
                    if sym:
                        a = symmetrize(a, 1.0)
                    c = fast_convolution(a)
                    S = float(a.sum())
                    score = float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(c)) / (S * S))
                    loga = _logarray(a)
                    loga -= float(np.mean(loga))
                    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                    t_adam = 0
                    lr = 0.10
                    stagnant = 0
                    no_improve = 0
                continue

            hu = h / rms
            t_adam += 1
            m = b1 * m + (1.0 - b1) * hu
            v = b2 * v + (1.0 - b2) * (hu * hu)

            bias1 = 1.0 - b1 ** t_adam
            bias2 = 1.0 - b2 ** t_adam
            step = lr * (m / bias1) / (np.sqrt(v / bias2) + eps_adam)
            step = np.where(np.isfinite(step), step, 0.0)
            step = np.clip(step, -max_step, max_step)

        accepted = False
        for scale in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125):
            loga2 = loga - scale * step
            loga2 -= float(np.max(loga2))
            a2 = np.exp(loga2)
            if sym:
                a2 = symmetrize(a2, 1.0)
            a2 = normalize_to_sum(a2, 1.0)

            if not np.all(np.isfinite(a2)):
                continue

            c2 = fast_convolution(a2)
            S2 = float(a2.sum())
            if S2 <= 0.0 or c2.size == 0:
                continue

            maxc2 = float(np.max(c2))
            score2 = float(2.0 * n * maxc2 / (S2 * S2))
            if not np.isfinite(score2):
                continue

            P2 = float(np.dot(w, c2))
            C2 = P2 / (S2 * S2)
            Ccur = P / (S * S)

            if score2 <= score * (1.0 + accept_tol) or (C2 < Ccur * (1.0 - 1e-6) and score2 <= score * 1.002):
                accepted = True
                a = a2
                c = c2
                S = S2
                score = score2
                loga = _logarray(a)
                loga -= float(np.mean(loga))
                break

        if accepted:
            if score < best_score:
                best = a.copy()
                best_score = score
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1
            lr = min(0.25, lr * 1.02)
            stagnant = 0
        else:
            lr = max(0.008, lr * 0.75)
            stagnant += 1
            if stagnant > 120:
                a = smooth_perturbation(best, rng, 0.22)
                if sym:
                    a = symmetrize(a, 1.0)
                c = fast_convolution(a)
                S = float(a.sum())
                score = float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(c)) / (S * S)) if S > 0 else float("inf")
                loga = _logarray(a)
                loga -= float(np.mean(loga))
                m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                t_adam = 0
                lr = 0.10
                stagnant = 0
                no_improve = 0

        if no_improve > 2500:
            kick = rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, n)
            loga = loga + kick
            loga -= float(np.mean(loga))
            a = np.exp(loga)
            if sym:
                a = symmetrize(a, 1.0)
            a = normalize_to_sum(a, 1.0)
            c = fast_convolution(a)
            S = float(a.sum())
            score = float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(c)) / (S * S)) if S > 0 else float("inf")
            if not np.isfinite(score):
                a = best.copy()
                c = fast_convolution(a)
                S = float(a.sum())
                score = best_score
                loga = _logarray(a)
                loga -= float(np.mean(loga))
            else:
                loga = _logarray(a)
                loga -= float(np.mean(loga))
            no_improve = 0
            lr = min(0.15, lr * 1.4)

    return best, best_score


def run_length(n, budget_s, parents, external_best, seed):
    """Explore one sequence length and return best fast-score sequence."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        n = 512
    budget_s = max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + budget_s

    if budget_s < 3.0:
        if external_best is not None:
            seq = upsample_sequence(external_best, n)
            return seq, fast_score_array(seq)
        seq = normalize_to_sum(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), 1.0)
        return seq, fast_score_array(seq)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    parent_list = []
    if parents:
        parent_list.extend(parents[:3])
    if external_best is not None:
        parent_list.append(np.asarray(external_best, dtype=np.float64))

    starts = generate_starts(n, parent_list, rng, count=26)

    # Low-dimensional basis refinement, only when worthwhile.
    basis_budget = 0.0
    if budget_s > 180.0 and n <= 2048:
        basis_budget = min(0.22 * budget_s, 420.0 if n <= 1024 else 260.0)
    if basis_budget > 35.0:
        bres = basis_search(n, basis_budget, parent_list, rng)
        for sc, a in bres:
            try_add_start(starts, a, n)
            try_add_start(starts, symmetrize(a, 1.0), n)

    if not starts:
        seq = normalize_to_sum(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), 1.0)
        return seq, fast_score_array(seq)

    starts.sort(key=pool_key)

    best_seq = None
    best_score = float("inf")
    best_sym_seq = None
    best_sym_score = float("inf")

    for sc, a in starts:
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_seq = a.copy()

        if np.isfinite(sc):
            b = symmetrize(a, 1.0)
            scb = fast_score_array(b)
            if np.isfinite(scb) and scb < best_sym_score:
                best_sym_score = float(scb)
                best_sym_seq = b.copy()

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = normalize_to_sum(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), 1.0)
        best_score = fast_score_array(best_seq)

    remain = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if remain < 10.0:
        return best_seq, best_score

    if remain > 260.0:
        phase_defs = [
            (True, "fixed", 0.34),
            (False, "adam", 0.31),
            (True, "adam", 0.18),
            (False, "fixed", 0.17),
        ]
    elif remain > 90.0:
        phase_defs = [
            (True, "fixed", 0.50),
            (False, "adam", 0.50),
        ]
    else:
        phase_defs = [
            (True, "fixed", 1.00),
        ]

    phase_budgets = []
    for _, _, frac in phase_defs:
        phase_budgets.append(max(0.0, remain * frac))

    for idx, ((do_sym, kind, _), pb) in enumerate(zip(phase_defs, phase_budgets)):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= deadline - 5.0:
            break
        pb = min(pb, max(0.0, deadline - now - 2.0))
        if pb < 5.0:
            continue

        if do_sym:
            if best_sym_seq is not None:
                start = best_sym_seq
            elif best_seq is not None:
                start = symmetrize(best_seq, 1.0)
            else:
                start = normalize_to_sum(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), 1.0)
        else:
            if best_seq is not None:
                start = best_seq
            else:
                start = normalize_to_sum(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), 1.0)

        try:
            seq, score = gradient_log_search(
                start,
                pb,
                seed=int(seed + 1000 + idx * 97),
                sym=do_sym,
                step_kind=kind,
                p_start=2.0,
                p_end=10.0,
            )
        except Exception:
            seq, score = start, fast_score_array(start)

        if seq is not None:
            seq = normalize_to_sum(np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64), 1.0)
            sc = fast_score_array(seq)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                if sc < best_score:
                    best_score = float(sc)
                    best_seq = seq.copy()

                # Update symmetric reference.
                if do_sym:
                    if sc < best_sym_score:
                        best_sym_score = float(sc)
                        best_sym_seq = seq.copy()
                else:
                    if sc < best_sym_score + 1e-12:
                        b = symmetrize(seq, 1.0)
                        scb = fast_score_array(b)
                        if np.isfinite(scb) and scb < best_sym_score:
                            best_sym_score = float(scb)
                            best_sym_seq = b.copy()

    if best_sym_seq is not None:
        scs = fast_score_array(best_sym_seq)
        if np.isfinite(scs) and scs < best_score:
            best_score = float(scs)
            best_seq = best_sym_seq.copy()

    return best_seq, best_score


def sanitize_for_return(seq):
    """Return a valid Python list of non-negative finite floats with sum 1."""
    a = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return [1.0 / 512.0] * 512

    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, _MAXELL)

    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return [1.0 / 512.0] * 512

    a = a / s
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, _MAXELL)

    if float(a.sum()) <= 1e-15:
        return [1.0 / 512.0] * 512

    return [float(v) for v in a]


def exact_score_list(lst):
    """Safe wrapper around the externally available evaluator."""
    try:
        return float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def final_select(pool, deadline):
    """Exact-validate a few top fast-score candidates and transformations."""
    pool.sort(key=pool_key)
    best_seq = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    max_calls = 10
    calls = 0

    for idx, (_fastsc, a) in enumerate(pool[:6]):
        if calls >= max_calls:
            break
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 1.0:
            break

        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        transforms = [a]

        if idx < 3:
            transforms.append(symmetrize(a, 1.0))
            transforms.append(zero_small(a, 1e-7))
        if idx < 2:
            transforms.append(zero_small(symmetrize(a, 1.0), 1e-7))

        for b in transforms:
            if calls >= max_calls:
                break
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 1.0:
                break
            try:
                lst = sanitize_for_return(b)
                ex = evaluate_sequence(lst)
                calls += 1
                ex = float(ex)
                if np.isfinite(ex) and ex < best_score:
                    best_score = ex
                    best_seq = lst
            except Exception:
                pass

    if best_seq is None:
        # Guaranteed valid fallback.
        fallback = sanitize_for_return(np.ones(512, dtype=np.float64) / 512.0)
        try:
            ex = evaluate_sequence(fallback)
            ex = float(ex)
        except Exception:
            ex = 2.0
        if np.isfinite(ex):
            best_score = ex
        else:
            best_score = 2.0
        best_seq = fallback

    return best_seq, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=12345, budget_s=2400.0, **kwargs):
    """
    Main entry point.

    Returns a list of non-negative float heights minimizing the evaluator.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = min(2400.0, max(0.0, float(budget_s)))
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0

    total_deadline = t0 + max(0.0, budget) - 2.0

    # Initial parent access.
    parent_arr = None
    try:
        parent_obj = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if parent_obj is not None:
            parent_arr = np.asarray(parent_obj, dtype=np.float64)
            if parent_arr.size > 0:
                parent_arr = normalize_to_sum(parent_arr, 1.0)
            else:
                parent_arr = None
    except Exception:
        parent_arr = None

    # Very low budget: return the best known or a safe uniform sequence.
    if budget < 30.0:
        if parent_arr is not None:
            return sanitize_for_return(parent_arr)
        return sanitize_for_return(np.ones(512, dtype=np.float64) / 512.0)

    reserve = 60.0 if budget > 600.0 else max(8.0, 0.10 * budget)
    search_deadline = t0 + max(10.0, budget - reserve)

    parent_seqs = []
    if parent_arr is not None:
        parent_seqs.append(parent_arr)

    print(f"Search start: budget={budget:.1f}s parent_len="
          f"{int(parent_arr.size) if parent_arr is not None else 0}", flush=True)

    # Choose base length.
    if parent_arr is not None:
        parent_len = int(parent_arr.size)
        if 256 <= parent_len <= 2048:
            base_n = parent_len
        else:
            base_n = 512
    else:
        base_n = 512

    length_set = {int(base_n)}
    if int(base_n) * 2 <= 2048:
        length_set.add(int(base_n) * 2)
    if int(base_n) > 256 and budget > 1200.0:
        length_set.add(max(128, int(base_n) // 2))
    if int(base_n) == 512 and budget > 2000.0:
        length_set.add(768)

    capacities = {}
    for n in length_set:
        if n == int(base_n):
            capacities[n] = 1.00
        elif n > int(base_n):
            capacities[n] = 0.78
        else:
            capacities[n] = 0.42

    # Ordered lengths: base first, then larger, then smaller.
    ordered = [int(base_n)]
    for n in sorted(length_set):
        if n > int(base_n) and n not in ordered:
            ordered.append(n)
    for n in sorted(length_set):
        if n < int(base_n) and n not in ordered:
            ordered.append(n)

    pool = []
    if parent_arr is not None:
        add_candidate_pool(pool, parent_arr, fast_score_array(parent_arr))

    # Quick safe candidate.
    add_candidate_pool(pool, np.ones(base_n, dtype=np.float64), 2.0)

    global_best_seq = pool[0][1] if pool else None

    total_search_time = max(0.0, search_deadline - time.monotonic())
    weight_sum = float(sum(capacities[n] for n in ordered))
    if weight_sum <= 0.0:
        weight_sum = 1.0

    for li, n in enumerate(ordered):
        if time.monotonic() >= search_deadline - 10.0:
            break

        b = total_search_time * (capacities.get(n, 0.5) / weight_sum)
        # Never let a non-base length suffer; ensure minimum useful time if available.
        if b < 45.0 and li > 0 and total_search_time > 120.0:
            b = min(180.0, total_search_time * 0.12)

        if time.monotonic() + b >= search_deadline - 5.0:
            b = max(5.0, search_deadline - time.monotonic() - 5.0)
        if b < 5.0:
            continue

        print(f"Length {n}: budget {b:.1f}s", flush=True)
        try:
            seq, score = run_length(
                n,
                b,
                parent_seqs,
                global_best_seq,
                seed=int(seed + 777 + li * 101),
            )
        except Exception:
            seq, score = None, float("inf")

        if seq is not None and np.isfinite(score):
            seq = normalize_to_sum(seq, 1.0)
            add_candidate_pool(pool, seq, score)
            if pool:
                pool.sort(key=pool_key)
                global_best_seq = pool[0][1].copy()
                print(f"Length {n} best fast score={score:.9f}; "
                      f"current global fast score={pool[0][0]:.9f}", flush=True)

    # Ensure fallback exists.
    add_candidate_pool(pool, np.ones(512, dtype=np.float64), 2.0)

    if not pool:
        return sanitize_for_return(np.ones(512, dtype=np.float64) / 512.0)

    pool.sort(key=pool_key)
    final_deadline = max(time.monotonic() + 5.0, total_deadline)
    best_seq, best_exact = final_select(pool, final_deadline)

    print(f"Final exact score (selected): {best_exact:.9f}", flush=True)

    return best_seq
```
